# Documents

Every written document in the Drive folder and where it stands against the repo:

- **in repo**: the same text is already in the repo (linked).
- **in repo (small differences)**: at least 90% of the lines are already in the repo (linked); the rest are small edits such as renamed characters. Not copied again.
- **partly in repo** / **different version**: some of the text is in the repo (linked), or the repo has a file with this name or title, but the Drive text differs a lot. The Drive text is copied under `docs/drive_index/docs/` so nothing is lost; someone who knows the story should pick the one to keep.
- **new**: not in the repo yet; copied under `docs/drive_index/docs/`.
- **bought pack file**: instructions, manifests and licences that came with a bought pack. Listed, not copied.
- **chat log** / **has a key or token**: raw chat logs, and any file with a download key, token or password in it. Listed, never copied into the repo.
- **image board**: a Word file that is mostly pictures; open it in Drive.

| Status | Documents |
|---|---:|
| in repo | 376 |
| new | 293 |
| partly in repo | 66 |
| image board | 36 |
| bought pack file | 30 |
| different version | 5 |
| chat log | 2 |
| has a key or token | 2 |
